## 3. Top 5 Culturally Sensitive / Nuanced Strings Flagged for Native Review

These 5 strings carry deep philosophical, spiritual, or commercial weight and have been translated with high care. Reviewers should confirm the tone aligns with local spiritual and yoga communities:

| # | Focus Area | English Source | Target Translation | Back-Translation | Linguistic Review Rationale |
| :-: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **1-Year Post-Graduation Mentorship Promise** (`home.whyChoose.postTtcDesc`) | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | La plupart des écoles disent au revoir le jour de la remise des diplômes. Pas nous. Pendant les 12 mois suivant votre formation, vous bénéficiez de sessions mensuelles en direct avec nous – des retours sur vos vrais cours si vous enseignez, des conseils pour votre pratique personnelle si vous n'enseignez pas – et un enseignant à qui vous pouvez écrire dès que vous hésitez. Ce n'est pas un argument marketing inventé : les étudiants des promotions passées de notre fondateur continuent de pratiquer en ligne avec lui trois, quatre ou cinq ans après leur cursus. Nous avons simplement formalisé un engagement qui existait déjà. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Expresses the school's core 12-month mentorship commitment with monthly live video guidance and personal feedback. |
| **2** | **Student Manifesto / Pure Practice Nuance** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoQuote`) | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | « Je ne souhaite pas vraiment enseigner. Je veux simplement approfondir ma pratique. » | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | High philosophical nuance distinguishing commercial fitness certification from traditional authentic sadhana transmission. |
| **3** | **Philosophy of Self-Discovery (Non-Career Focus)** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoIntro`) | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Plus de la moitié des personnes qui nous écrivent disent cela, presque en s'excusant. Ne vous excusez surtout pas. En Inde, le yoga n'a jamais été une formation professionnelle – c'était une voie de connaissance de soi. L'élève a toujours été au premier plan. | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Reframing yoga teacher training as personal self-realization rather than a commercial career course. |
| **4** | **Sacred Vedic Fire Ceremony (Agni Hotra)** (`course200.inclusions.incCeremonies`) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Cérémonies traditionnelles du feu d'ouverture et de clôture (Agni Hotra) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Preservation of ancient Vedic sacred fire purification rituals and spiritual tradition. |
| **5** | **Sacred Waterfall Cleansing Ceremony (Melukat)** (`course200.excursionsList[2].description`) | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Rendez-vous auprès d'une cascade sacrée pour participer au rituel traditionnel de purification par l'eau pure. | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Balinese Hindu purification ritual at a sacred holy water temple; requires deep cultural respect and sacred phrasing. |

| Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | La plupart des écoles disent au revoir le jour de la remise des diplômes. Pas nous. Pendant les 12 mois suivant votre formation, vous bénéficiez de sessions mensuelles en direct avec nous – des retours sur vos vrais cours si vous enseignez, des conseils pour votre pratique personnelle si vous n'enseignez pas – et un enseignant à qui vous pouvez écrire dès que vous hésitez. Ce n'est pas un argument marketing inventé : les étudiants des promotions passées de notre fondateur continuent de pratiquer en ligne avec lui trois, quatre ou cinq ans après leur cursus. Nous avons simplement formalisé un engagement qui existait déjà. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. | 200hr Bali: overview | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Notre formation de professeur de yoga 200 heures à Bali est un cursus complet pour débutants et intermédiaires désireux de devenir professeurs certifiés ou d'approfondir le yoga dans leur vie quotidienne. YogaGarhi est situé à Gianyar, à seulement 15 km d'Ubud, au cœur d'un environnement villageois verdoyant. Au cours de la formation, vous pratiquerez quotidiennement les asanas, apprendrez l'alignement précis, et étudierez le pranayama, la méditation, la philosophie et la pédagogie d'enseignement. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within FR grammatical structure. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Vous vous demandez si vous êtes vraiment prêt pour une formation de professeur de yoga (TTC) ? Ce programme préparatoire est spécialement conçu pour tester votre préparation. Alors que la plupart des écoles commencent le premier jour, nous débutons plusieurs semaines avant – en ligne et entièrement gratuitement – pour vous aider à bâtir des bases solides afin d'arriver serein, ancré et confiant. | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? This preparatory program is designed specifically for practitioners who want to test their readiness. While most schools start on day one, we begin weeks earlier — online and completely free — helping you build a solid foundation so you arrive confident, grounded, and stress-free. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | 1-Year Post-TTC Support | Soutien post-TTC pendant 1 an | 1-Year Post-TTC Support | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |
| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Your Teacher Stays With You For One Year | Votre enseignant reste à vos côtés pendant un an | Your Teacher Stays With You For One Year | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |
| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | La plupart des écoles disent au revoir le jour de la remise des diplômes. Pas nous. Pendant les 12 mois suivant votre formation, vous bénéficiez de sessions mensuelles en direct avec nous – des retours sur vos vrais cours si vous enseignez, des conseils pour votre pratique personnelle si vous n'enseignez pas – et un enseignant à qui vous pouvez écrire dès que vous hésitez. Ce n'est pas un argument marketing inventé : les étudiants des promotions passées de notre fondateur continuent de pratiquer en ligne avec lui trois, quatre ou cinq ans après leur cursus. Nous avons simplement formalisé un engagement qui existait déjà. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 100hr Bali YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Notre formation de professeur de yoga 200 heures à Bali est un cursus complet pour débutants et intermédiaires désireux de devenir professeurs certifiés ou d'approfondir le yoga dans leur vie quotidienne. | 300hr Bali Advanced YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Notre formation de professeur de yoga 200 heures à Bali est un cursus complet pour débutants et intermédiaires désireux de devenir professeurs certifiés ou d'approfondir le yoga dans leur vie quotidienne. YogaGarhi est situé à Gianyar, à seulement 15 km d'Ubud, au cœur d'un environnement villageois verdoyant. Au cours de la formation, vous pratiquerez quotidiennement les asanas, apprendrez l'alignement précis, et étudierez le pranayama, la méditation, la philosophie et la pédagogie d'enseignement. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 100hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Notre formation de professeur de yoga 200 heures à Bali est un cursus complet pour débutants et intermédiaires désireux de devenir professeurs certifiés ou d'approfondir le yoga dans leur vie quotidienne. YogaGarhi est situé à Gianyar, à seulement 15 km d'Ubud, au cœur d'un environnement villageois verdoyant. | 200hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Notre formation de professeur de yoga 200 heures à Bali est un cursus complet pour débutants et intermédiaires désireux de devenir professeurs certifiés ou d'approfondir le yoga dans leur vie quotidienne. YogaGarhi est situé à Gianyar, à seulement 15 km d'Ubud, au cœur d'un environnement villageois verdoyant. Au cours de la formation, vous pratiquerez quotidiennement les asanas, apprendrez l'alignement précis, et étudierez le pranayama, la méditation, la philosophie et la pédagogie d'enseignement. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life.
